  "chunk_text": "Methodology\n\nList of highly cited retracted articles were retrieved using \"Retraction Watch\", devoted to the\nexamination of retracted articles as \"a window into scientific process\".   "chunk_text": "He argued that the research tradition and culture in developing countries seems to be the \nbyproduct of the global framework of international capitalist relations.   "section_heading": "B.",
  "chunk_text": "B.\n\nMake reading fun:\n\nReading aloud can be a lot of fun, not just for parents but for all family members.   "section_heading": "2012 Amendments to Indian Copyright Act 1957",
